### Future CI integration pattern

Each d-test integration PR follows the **d058 sister-pattern** (set by PR #511):

1. **Workflow addition:** Add a new step (or new job) to `.github/workflows/lint-and-test.yml` for the specific d-test.
2. **SHA pin:** All `actions/*` references use SHA-pinned versions (lens h per ADR-0043).
3. **9-Lens (i) 8 sub-categories:** Document trigger / timeout / permissions / concurrency / secrets / network / artifact / Layer 5 race awareness per ADR-0043.
4. **INDEX.md registration:** Add a new entry to this file under the "ACTIVE" section (move from "planned" table).
5. **PR contract:** Draft PR with 4-cat labels per ADR-0012 + dual-channel `scripts/peer-poke.sh` peer notification per ADR-0033 (post-S28-008 LEGACY-REMOVE; was `scripts/ping.sh` pre-S28-008).

| **Doctrinal origin** | Issue #809 surfaced 2026-07-04T08:46Z by orchestrator peer-poke observation: 3 claim invocations on same #796 within 26s. RCA: `scripts/claim-next-ready.sh` is a read-then-write sequence (WIP count query → ready items query → status:ready → status:in-progress flip). Two processes executing steps 1-3 concurrently both observe the same WIP count (stale read), both pick the same `picked_number`, both flip it, both comment "claimed". Fix per ADR-0038 §Auto-Claim Protocol integrity: wrap critical section in `flock -n` (non-blocking flock), per-role lock file `/var/lock/dev-studio/claim-${ROLE}.lock`. Concurrent invocations fail-fast with exit 5 instead of racing. Lock file override via `CLAIM_NEXT_READY_LOCK_FILE` env var (used by d809 TC4 + emergency manual cleanup). Critical implementation detail per d031 TC5 sister-pattern guard: subshell exit code must be propagated via `exit $?` — unconditional `exit 0` would mask d031 TC4 WIP-cap exit 3 and break sister-test non-regression. Cadence Rule 1 atomic (ADR-0055 §1) preserved: d809 file + INDEX.md row + impl in claim-next-ready.sh + d031 sister non-regression all atomic in same PR cluster. Issue cluster: #806 (silent-drop, sibling class — both Auto-Claim Protocol integrity) + #809 (read-then-write race, this impl) + #811 (Form C verdict-stamp race, sibling-fix in PR #823, sister-class). |

| **Doctrinal origin** | Cycle ~#3671 dev escalation surfaced the bug: PR #796 (tester RED-state capture Cluster A, agent:tester + verdict-by:future 2026-07-04T04:31:52Z) and PR #797 (tester Sprint 24 prep test plans, agent:tester + verdict-by:future 2026-07-04T04:47:58Z) — both are tester-owned PRs in owner squash queue, with verdict-by stamps from peer review requests. The stale_verdict watcher function (scripts/agent-watch.sh:1085-1142) used `gh pr list --label "cc:${ROLE}"` to fetch candidates, then queried for `verdict-by:passed`. This conflates two distinct label semantics per ADR-0015: `cc:<role>` is the INFORMATIONAL lane (queue-passing, no verdict authority) while `agent:<role>` is OWNERSHIP (verdict authority). When tester-owned PR #796 + #797 entered the owner squash queue with peer `cc:developer` + `cc:human` + `verdict-by:future` labels, the watcher fired false-positive stale_verdict events on the tester pane (cc:developer requesting verdict is informational lane only — tester has cc:developer but no verdict authority on own PRs). Fix per dev cycle ~#3671: filter stale_verdict to fire only on (agent:<role> AND verdict-by:*) OR (cc:human AND verdict-by:*) — excludes the (cc:<peer> AND verdict-by:*) false-positive class. Tester-side d-test authored per ADR-0044 RED-first + ADR-0049 ≥3-TC sister-pattern; arch-side sister-test d320 covers the same bug class from the watch-query shape regression perspective. Both d-tests are sister-cycle per ADR-0049 §sister-pattern lineage. **d-test number slot rationale per Issue #113 + ADR-0055 §1**: d117-d122 reserved for Sprint 23 P1+P2 cluster (RC-17 + RC-20 + AC4 etc.), d123 reserved for upcoming sister-test (Sprint 24 prep), d124 = next free slot for Issue #798 sister-test authoring. |
